Sign in to load shared sponsorship records.
Sponsorships are relationship-based. This workspace is for repeatable cash sponsors and renewal follow-up. Gift cards, merchandise, raffle items, and other one-off in-kind donations stay in the Bikes for Tykes Tracking Tab and do not enter the sponsor renewal pipeline.

Sponsorship Dashboard

Cash received by year

Master sponsor relationships

One line per sponsor. Cash gifts are rolled up across years so you can see who gave, how much they have given, and who needs the next ask.

Legacy Sponsor Register

Historical sponsor records are preserved here while the relationship-based register becomes the working renewal system.

Documents Requested

This section will track W-9s, sponsorship letters, receipts, logos, agreements, and other requested documents.

Renewal follow-ups

Log each conversation, set the next follow-up, and record a new contribution when a sponsor renews. Pledges remain separate from money received.
